- **kount-device-data-collector-script-404-c6a134f51cc19dfb**: Fixes Kount Device Data Collector pages that 404 after a CDN or domain change: points the collector at the current Kount hosted URL for your merchant, updates content security policy, and verifies device data reaches RIS. Use when the collector script fails to load and device fields arrive empty. Not for fingerprint quality problems where the script loads and data flows. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kount-device-data-collector-script-404-c6a134f51cc19dfb))
- **kount-email-transactions**: Capture Gmail INBOX financial messages in Kount as PENDING, reconcile duplicates, archive labeled promotions as read, and optionally notify Slack with a short summary. - **kount-error-201-invalid-merchant-id-f25cacadb41bc92f**: Fixes Kount error 201 on the RIS API: the merchant id in the request does not match the authenticated account. Use when every RIS call returns 201 from a fresh integration or right after a credential rotation. Not for DECLINE_REVIEW timeouts or response parsing errors. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/kount-error-201-invalid-merchant-id-f25cacadb41bc92f))

- **kount-mail-setup**: Configure Gmail-to-Kount processing through a short interview about inbox scope, labels, read state, financial capture and optional Slack summaries. - **kount-pending-review**: Review Kount PENDING captures and propose evidence-based corrections without confirming them. ([TechincMX/kount-plugins](https://github.com/TechincMX/kount-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/kount/skills/kount-pending-review))
- **kount-recurring-charge-review**: Find possible recurring Kount charges and amount changes without automatically creating subscriptions or payments. ([TechincMX/kount-plugins](https://github.com/TechincMX/kount-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/kount/skills/kount-recurring-charge-review))
- **kount-ris-error-702-authentication-fai-632814e8f323a9a2**: Fixes Kount RIS error 702 authentication failures: verifies the merchant id, API key, and endpoint environment pairing, and corrects test versus production credential mixups.
